x="294.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">FARMAKODYNAMIKA — co dělá lék s tělem: mechanismus účinku a vztah</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" x="294.0" y="828.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Zdroje rostlinné (morfin, atropin, digoxin), živočišné (heparin,</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">inzulin), mikrobiální (peniciliny), chemická syntéza a</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">biotechnologie (monoklonální protilátky).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="896.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="902.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Tři visačky</text><text x="294.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Názvy: chemický (vzorec) · generický = mezinárodní nechráněný</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(ibuprofen) · firemní chráněný (Brufen®).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="948.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="954.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Červená kniha</text><text x="294.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">LÉKOPIS — závazný soubor požadavků na jakost léčiv; Český vychází z</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Evropského.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1001.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1007.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Lékárnický kotlík</text><text x="294.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Magistraliter se připravuje v lékárně podle receptu; HVLP se vyrábí</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">hromadně.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1054.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1060.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1059.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Hotová tableta</text><text x="294.0" y="1059.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Léčivá LÁTKA je nositel účinku, léčivý PŘÍPRAVEK je hotová forma,</text><text x="294.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">kterou pacient dostane.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1105.8" width="846.0" height="62.5" pultu</text><text x="294.0" y="750.6" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">LÉČIVÝ PŘÍPRAVEK působí farmakologicky, imunologicky nebo</text><text x="294.0" y="770.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">metabolicky; musí prokázat účinnost, bezpečnost a jakost.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="798.3" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="804.3" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="803.3" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Kelímek na druhém pultu</text><text x="294.0" y="803.3" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">DOPLNĚK STRAVY je právně potravina — účinnost dokládat nemusí,</text><text x="294.0" y="823.6" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">stačí ohlášení, a nesmí tvrdit, že léčí. Dozor má SZPI.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="850.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="856.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="855.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Náplast na třetím pultu</text><text x="294.0" y="855.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ZDRAVOTNICKÝ PROSTŘEDEK působí FYZIKÁLNĚ (výplň, implantát, obvaz)</text><text x="294.0" y="876.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">— posuzuje se shoda a riziková třída.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="903.6" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="909.6" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="908.6" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Úřední budova</text><text x="294.0" y="908.6" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">SÚKL registruje léčiva, stanovuje ceny a úhrady, dozoruje lékárny,</text><text x="294.0" y="928.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">povoluje klinická hodnocení a vede farmakovigilanci.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="956.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="962.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="961.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Vlajka nad tržnicí</text><text x="294.0" y="961.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">EMA vede centralizovanou registraci platnou pro celou EU.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="988.6" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="994.6" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="993.6" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Modrý pruh na krabičce</text><text x="294.0" y="993.6" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Omamné a psychotropní látky se předepisují na recept s modrým</text><text x="294.0" y="1013.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">pruhem a mají přísnější evidenci.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1041.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1047.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1046.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Podání ruky</text><text x="294.0" y="1046.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Klinické hodnocení musí kromě SÚKL schválit i etická komise.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1072.5" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M31.9,1073.2 L866.0,1069.7 Q872.0,1073.7 871.1,1077.3 L871.7,1129.3 Q872.3,1136.1 869.5,1133.1 L34.3,1138.3 Q26.3,1138.5 25.2,1126.7 872.7,1077.4 L875.2,1129.2 Q872.7,1134.5 866.2,1136.9 L34.4,1133.0 Q23.2,1137.2 25.2,1128.0 L23.6,1080.3 Q28.6,1068.5 30.0,1073.6" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1093.5" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1093.5" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Pacient rozdíl mezi lékem a doplňkem nevidí — na doplňky se proto ptej cíleně: třezalka, ginkgo a</text><text x="66.0" y="1112.8" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">česnek mají reálné interakce.</text></svg>

fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="723.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Dva podpisy pod receptem</text><text x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Recept je právní dokument se sdílenou odpovědností — proto musí být</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">čitelný a úplný.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="770.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="776.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Obrazovka s eReceptem</text><text x="294.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">eRecept je standard: lékař ho vystaví do centrálního úložiště,</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">pacient dostane identifikátor. Listinný recept zůstává pro výpadky.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="823.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="829.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="828.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Průkazka pacienta</text><text x="294.0" y="828.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Náležitosti: pacient (jméno, číslo pojištěnce) · léčivo (název,</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">síla, forma, množství) · dávkování a způsob podání (D.S.) · lékař a</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">pracoviště s podpisem a razítkem · datum.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="896.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="902.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Modrý pruh</text><text x="294.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Vyhrazen omamným a psychotropním látkám; přísnější evidence.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="928.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="934.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="933.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Třecí miska</text><text x="294.0" y="933.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Magistraliter předpis má stavbu Rp. (recipe) — složení — M.f.</text><text x="294.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(misce fiat) — D.S. (da signa).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="981.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="987.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="986.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Kalendář na zdi</text><text x="294.0" y="986.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Platnost běžného receptu je zpravidla 14 dní, u antibiotik kratší</text><text x="294.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">[⚠️ ověřit dle skript]. Opakovací recept uvádí počet opakování.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1033.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1039.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1038.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Výměna krabiček</text><text x="294.0" y="1038.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Generická substituce — lékárník smí vydat jiný přípravek se stejnou</text><text x="294.0" y="1059.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">účinnou látkou, silou a formou, pokud to lékař nezakázal a pacient</text><text x="294.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">souhlasí.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1105.8" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M29.0,1106.7 L868.2,1104.9 Q872.6,1104.4 871.1,1108.4 L875.2,1165.8 Q872.4,1172.7 867.2,1167.9 L32.1,1172.4 Q24.8,1167.3 23.8,1166.1 L28.5,1108.1 Q25.9,1102.3 35.3,1106.0" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M31.3,1102.3 L867.9,1103.9 Q872.4,1105.8 876.2,1112.4 L871.5,1160.1 Q872.2,1170.7 867.1,1171.0 L33.2,1172.6 Q29.0,1167.8 23.8,1165.7 L25.5,1113.3 Q23.5,1105.5 31.6,1106.6" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1126.8" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1126.8" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Před předepsáním patří anamnéza: alergie, gravidita a kojení, funkce jater a ledvin a všechny ostatní</text><text x="66.0" y="1146.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">léky včetně volně prodejných.</text></svg>

font-size="13.5" font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">CO JE VE SCÉNĚ</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="717.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="723.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Myš na spodní příčce</text><text x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">PREKLINIKA in vitro a na zvířeti: farmakokinetika, farmakodynamika,</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">toxicita (ED50, TD50, LD50), mutagenita, teratogenita,</text><text x="294.0" y="763.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">karcinogenita.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="790.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="796.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Zdravý dobrovolník</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">FÁZE I — desítky ZDRAVÝCH: bezpečnost a snášenlivost; zahajuje se</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">mikrodávkami s pomalou eskalací.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="843.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="849.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Stovka zkumavek</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">FÁZE II — stovky nemocných: hledá se účinná dávka.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="875.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="881.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="880.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Zástup lidí</text><text x="294.0" y="880.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">FÁZE III — tisíce nemocných: srovnání s dosavadní léčbou nebo</text><text x="294.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">placebem; na jejím základě se registruje.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="928.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="934.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="933.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Zeměkoule nahoře</text><text x="294.0" y="933.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">FÁZE IV — po uvedení na trh, běžná populace. Teprve tady se odhalí</text><text x="294.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">VZÁCNÉ nežádoucí účinky, protože ve studii na tisících neměly šanci</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">vyjít najevo.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1001.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1007.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Kostka randomizace</text><text x="294.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Zásady studie: randomizace, zaslepení (jednoduché, dvojité,</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">trojité), kontrolní skupina, předem daný cíl, souhlas etické</text><text x="294.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">komise.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1074.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1080.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Kopírák</text><text x="294.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Generikum účinnost neprokazuje znovu — dokládá BIOEKVIVALENCI, tedy</text><text x="294.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">shodný průběh hladin s originálem.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1126.1" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M34.7,1122.8 L870.4,1126.3 Q875.1,1127.3 871.1,1134.4 L875.3,1181.2 Q870.9,1187.9 865.0,1186.7 L34.8,1189.0 Q24.5,1186.7 25.1,1185.9 L28.8,1133.0 Q23.3,1124.7 34.3,1124.7" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M30.6,1124.2 L866.1,1126.2 Q875.2,1122.3 873.0,1131.6 L872.8,1180.3 Q872.9,1189.7 869.4,1189.9 L35.1,1190.6 Q23.4,1189.0 25.8,1184.4 L23.5,1131.1 Q28.1,1124.9 29.0,1127.9" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1147.1" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1147.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Preklinika sama nestačí: zvíře nemá stejný metabolismus ani receptory. Od objevu k registraci uplyne</text><text x="66.0" y="1166.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">zpravidla 10–15 let.</text></svg>

x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">PERORÁLNĚ: látka projde střevní stěnou a játry dřív, než se dostane</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">do oběhu (first-pass). Proto se stejný lék podává ústy v</text><text x="294.0" y="763.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">mnohonásobně vyšší dávce než do žíly.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="790.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="796.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Odbočka pod jazykem</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">SUBLINGVÁLNĚ a bukálně — vstřebání rovnou do systémového oběhu,</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">obchází játra; proto nitroglycerin pod jazyk.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="843.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="849.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Stříkačka na cestě</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">I.V.: okamžitý účinek a biologická dostupnost 100 % — ale podané se</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nedá vzít zpět.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="896.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="902.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Sval u cesty</text><text x="294.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">I.M. rychlé, S.C. pomalejší (inzulin, hepariny); dále intratekálně,</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">intraartikulárně, intraoseálně. Vyžadují sterilitu a personál.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="948.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="954.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Náplast na plotě</text><text x="294.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">TRANSDERMÁLNĚ — nejpomalejší nástup, ale stálá hladina po dny; po</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">sejmutí účinek doznívá z kožního depa.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1001.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1007.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Zadní vrátka</text><text x="294.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">REKTÁLNĚ — funguje při zvracení a u dětí, játra obchází jen zčásti</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(dolní část konečníku), vstřebávání je kolísavé.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1054.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1060.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1059.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Oko nad krajinou</text><text x="294.0" y="1059.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">„Místní“ podání neznamená „bez celkového účinku“ — timolol z očních</text><text x="294.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">kapek vyvolá bradykardii a bronchospasmus.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1105.8" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M29.7,1106.3 L870.1,1103.0 Q874.0,1107.0 873.2,1112.6 L872.8,1160.9 Q876.4,1169.2 868.8,1167.5 L34.5,1171.5 Q27.9,1170.4 24.5,1166.3 L28.3,1111.9 Q27.9,1105.2 35.2,1101.5" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M30.4,1103.2 L867.5,1104.4 Q871.0,1101.5 872.6,1113.4 L876.2,1161.9 Q871.3,1172.5 866.7,1171.3 L33.2,1167.0 Q27.3,1171.0 23.8,1164.4 L27.3,1107.9 Q22.7,1106.0 29.8,1104.0" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1126.8" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1126.8" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Biologická dostupnost F je podíl dávky, který se dostane nezměněný do oběhu; i.v. má z definice F =</text><text x="66.0" y="1146.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">100 %.</text></svg>

fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="723.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Tableta na pultu</text><text x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">PERORÁLNÍ formy se polykají a působí až po vstřebání: tablety,</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">potahované tablety, tobolky, granuláty, sirupy, suspenze, kapky.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="770.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="776.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Pastilka ve sklenici</text><text x="294.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ORÁLNÍ formy zůstávají v ústech: pastilky, žvýkací tablety, ústní</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">vody, gely (per os = skrz ústa, oralis = ústní).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="823.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="829.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="828.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Štít na tabletě</text><text x="294.0" y="828.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ENTEROSOLVENTNÍ obal projde žaludkem a rozpadne se až ve střevě —</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">chrání látku před kyselinou nebo žaludek před látkou.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="875.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="881.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="880.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Cedule NEDRTIT</text><text x="294.0" y="880.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Retardované formy (SR, ZOK) uvolňují látku postupně — rozdrcením se</text><text x="294.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">uvolní celá denní dávka najednou nebo se látka zničí. Totéž platí</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">pro enterosolventní obal.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="948.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="954.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Jazyk pod pultem</text><text x="294.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Sublingválně a bukálně se látka vstřebá rovnou do krve a obejde</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">first-pass — nitroglycerin, buprenorfin.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1001.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1007.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Konvička s laktózou</text><text x="294.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Pomocné látky nejsou neutrální: laktóza vadí při intoleranci,</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">barviva mohou vyvolat alergii, cukr v sirupech je při dlouhodobé</text><text x="294.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">léčbě rizikový.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1074.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1080.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Rozpouštějící se kostka</text><text x="294.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Rozpad a rozpuštění předchází vstřebání — u málo rozpustných léčiv</text><text x="294.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">je rychlost rozpouštění krokem určujícím nástup účinku.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1126.1" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M30.6,1125.7 L868.0,1122.6 Q871.4,1127.3 871.2,1131.1 L872.2,1186.4 Q876.3,1193.0 fázi rozpadu tuhé lékové</text><text x="66.0" y="1166.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">formy.</text></svg>

font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Mýdlo a rouška</text><text x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Parenterální přípravek obchází kůži i sliznici, proto musí být</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">STERILNÍ, APYROGENNÍ, IZOTONICKÝ, IZOHYDRICKÝ a bez mechanických</text><text x="294.0" y="763.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nečistot.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="790.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="796.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Krevní řečiště</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Rychlost účinku sleduje prokrvení: i.v. okamžitě a se 100%</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">dostupností · i.m. rychle · s.c. pomaleji · depotní formy a</text><text x="294.0" y="836.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">náplasti nejpomaleji, ale působí dny až měsíce.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="863.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="869.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Přeškrtnutá kalná</text><text x="64.0" y="889.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">lahvička</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">SUSPENZE A EMULZE SE NIKDY NEPODÁVAJÍ I.V. — hrozí embolizace.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="916.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="922.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Váhy výhod a nevýhod</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Výhody: jistá dostupnost, přesné dávkování, použitelné u zvracení a</text><text x="294.0" y="941.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">bezvědomí. Nevýhody: bolestivost, riziko infekce a embolie, nutný</text><text x="294.0" y="962.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">personál a NEVRATNOST podání.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="989.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="995.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Řada kelímků</text><text x="294.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Dermatologika: mast (tučný základ, největší průnik, na suchou</text><text x="294.0" y="1014.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">kůži), krém (voda i tuk), gel (vodný, chladí), pasta, zásyp,</text><text x="294.0" y="1035.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">roztok, náplast. Průnik zvyšuje okluze.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1062.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1068.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1067.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Ztenčená kůže</text><text x="294.0" y="1067.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Lokální kortikoidy dlouhodobě způsobí atrofii kůže a strie — na</text><text x="294.0" y="1087.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">obličej a do záhybů jen slabé přípravky a krátce, protože se tam</text><text x="294.0" y="1108.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">vstřebávají nejvíc.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1135.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1141.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1140.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Náplast na paži</text><text x="294.0" y="1140.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Transdermální náplast obchází játra a udrží stálou hladinu po dny</text><text x="294.0" y="1160.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(fentanyl, nikotin, estrogeny).</text><rect x="27.0" y="1187.1" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M34.4,1186.9 L870.3,1185.5 Q872.0,1185.7 871.2,1189.9 L875.7,1243.7 Q876.4,1250.5 864.9,1249.0 L33.2,1247.9 Q28.0,1251.6 23.6,1245.0 L27.6,1191.2 Q26.1,1186.1 33.8,1188.6" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" Q29.4,1252.4 25.0,1245.9 L25.4,1188.9 Q29.4,1185.6 29.5,1189.4" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1208.1" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1208.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Depotní a implantabilní formy působí týdny až měsíce — výhodou je adherence, nevýhodou to, že se při</text><text x="66.0" y="1227.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">nežádoucí reakci nedají odstranit.</text></svg>

font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Oční kapky</text><text x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Musí být sterilní a izotonické, po otevření omezená použitelnost.</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">TIMOLOL z kapek vyvolá bradykardii a bronchospasmus; vstřebání</text><text x="294.0" y="763.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">sníží stisknutí vnitřního koutku po nakapání.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="790.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="796.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Nosní sprej</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Dekongescencia (xylometazolin) jen 5–7 DNÍ, jinak vzniká rhinitis</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">medicamentosa. Nosní cestou se podává i desmopresin a sumatriptan k</text><text x="294.0" y="836.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">celkovému účinku.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="863.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="869.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Ušní kapátko</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Ušní kapky se nesmí podat při perforaci bubínku.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="896.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="902.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Inhalátor s nástavcem</text><text x="294.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Aerosolový dávkovač, práškový inhalátor, nebulizace. Rozhoduje</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">TECHNIKA inhalace; nástavec zlepší depozici a sníží orofaryngeální</text><text x="294.0" y="941.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">kandidózu a chrapot po inhalačním kortikoidu.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="969.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="975.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Čípek</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Rektalia obcházejí játra jen z dolní části konečníku, vstřebávání</text><text x="294.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">je kolísavé; hodí se u zvracení, u dětí a v bezvědomí (diazepam u</text><text x="294.0" y="1014.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">křečí).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1042.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1048.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Vaginální globule</text><text x="294.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Slouží hlavně k místní léčbě (antimykotika, estrogeny), ale i odsud</text><text x="294.0" y="1067.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">se látka částečně vstřebává.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1094.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1100.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Cedule nade vším</text><text x="294.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Společné pravidlo: všechny tyto formy se vstřebávají a mohou</text><text x="294.0" y="1120.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">působit celkově.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1146.5" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M33.4,1148.8 L865.6,1146.0 Q874.5,1147.6 871.3,1148.2 L875.9,1205.0 Q874.9,1211.5 869.5,1206.8 L29.1,1210.2 Q27.7,1210.1 24.8,1203.8 L27.8,1148.3 Q23.5,1147.5 32.8,1146.2" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M29.6,1144.5 L871.0,1148.0 Q873.1,1143.7 873.7,1153.0 L874.9,1202.5 Q873.3,1213.2 868.9,1209.0 L29.7,1212.2 Q23.8,1212.7 27.1,1203.6 L23.4,1152.6 Q27.2,1144.0 33.7,1142.9" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1167.5" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1167.5" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Nosní a bukální sliznice jsou dobře prokrvené a first-pass obcházejí — vstřebání odsud může být</text><text x="66.0" y="1186.7" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">rychlejší než ze střeva.</text></svg>

y="312.0" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">6</text><ellipse cx="685.9" cy="147.2" rx="22.1" ry="5.8" fill="#23303A" opacity="0.12"/><text x="685.9" y="149.5" font-size="58.17425834618509" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="middle">🗣️</text><circle cx="709.2" cy="110.0" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="709.2" y="116.0" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">7</text></g><path d="M36.0,186.0 L866.4,186.0 Q872.2,185.7 874.8,192.5 L873.2,646.8 Q875.4,654.7 865.2,654.4 L33.6,653.9 L864.4,182.8 Q872.3,184.0 876.0,193.5 L873.4,647.5 Q875.6,652.8 866.3,652.4 L34.1,654.3 Q28.0,656.1 24.4,647.6 L25.1,192.7 Q25.8,185.7 34.3,184.3" fill="none" stroke="#23303A" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="26.0" y="687.9" font-size="13.5" font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">CO JE VE SCÉNĚ</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="717.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="723.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Naslouchající ucho</text><text x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">COMPLIANCE — míra, do jaké pacient dodržuje pokyny; pasivní pojetí.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="750.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="756.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="755.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Podání ruky</text><text x="294.0" y="755.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ADHERENCE — pacient se na plánu podílel; KONKORDANCE je společné</text><text x="294.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">rozhodnutí lékaře a pacienta. Dnes se preferuje adherence, protože</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">zdůrazňuje spoluodpovědnost.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="823.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="829.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="828.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Jiskra nad hlavou</text><text x="294.0" y="828.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">PLACEBO — očekávané zlepšení; má neurobiologický podklad (endogenní</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">opioidy, dopamin), není „vymyšlené“ a zesiluje účinek každého</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">skutečného léku.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="896.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="902.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Mrak s bleskem</text><text x="294.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">NOCEBO — očekávaná škoda; nepříznivé věty a příbalový leták</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">vyvolají potíž. Odsud časté vysazení statinů a antidepresiv.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="948.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="954.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Hromada krabiček</text><text x="294.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Nejčastější příčina selhání léčby není špatný lék, ale NEBRANÝ lék:</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">složitý režim, mnoho tablet, nežádoucí účinky a obavy z nich, cena,</text><text x="294.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nepochopení, nedůvěra.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1021.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1027.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Jedna tableta denně</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Zlepší to fixní kombinace (méně tablet), dávkování 1× denně,</text><text x="294.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">srozumitelné vysvětlení a kontrola.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1074.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1080.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Mluvící lékař</text><text x="294.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Formulace lékaře je sama o sobě účinná látka s vlastní dávkou —</text><text x="294.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">proto se způsob sdělení plánuje stejně jako dávka.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1126.1" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M30.4,1121.9 L867.9,1127.6 Q875.1,1124.7 871.3,1132.4 L872.6,1181.1 Q875.0,1187.7 867.2,1188.0 L33.7,1190.8 Q26.2,1192.1 23.5,1182.7 L27.1,1131.1 Q26.2,1123.0 29.7,1128.2" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M29.8,1122.6 L868.4,1126.5 Q872.6,1124.5 877.2,1134.5 L871.1,1186.6 Q872.9,1189.0 869.8,1189.6 L35.3,1191.3 Q22.8,1189.7 25.7,1180.9 L25.3,1131.9 Q28.9,1127.4 35.3,1122.3" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1147.1" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1147.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Placebo se neomezuje na placebo tablety — každý účinný lék nese i placebovou složku, a ta se sčítá s</text><text x="66.0" y="1166.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">jeho farmakologickým účinkem.</text></svg>

font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">CO JE VE SCÉNĚ</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="692.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="698.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Máslová zeď</text><text x="294.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Membrána je lipidová dvojvrstva — projde jen látka LIPOFILNÍ a</text><text x="294.0" y="718.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">NENABITÁ; ionizovaná forma neprojde.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="745.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="751.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="750.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Chodec po svahu</text><text x="294.0" y="750.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">PROSTÁ DIFUZE — po koncentračním spádu, bez energie a bez</text><text x="294.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">přenašeče; hlavní mechanismus u většiny léčiv.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="798.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="804.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="803.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Baterie u brány</text><text x="294.0" y="803.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">AKTIVNÍ TRANSPORT — proti spádu, spotřebuje ATP, je saturovatelný</text><text x="294.0" y="823.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(P-glykoprotein léčiva aktivně vypuzuje z buňky). Facilitovaná</text><text x="294.0" y="843.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">difuze jde přenašečem, ale po spádu a bez energie.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="871.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="877.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="876.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Past za zdí</text><text x="294.0" y="876.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">IONTOVÁ PAST: látka projde v nenabité formě, na druhé straně se při</text><text x="294.0" y="896.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">jiném pH nabije a zpět už neprojde — hromadí se. Proto alkalizace</text><text x="294.0" y="916.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">moči urychlí vyloučení salicylátů.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="944.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="950.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="949.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Hlídaná brána do mozku</text><text x="294.0" y="949.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Hematoencefalická bariéra — těsné spoje a P-glykoprotein. Zánět ji</text><text x="294.0" y="969.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">zpropustní: penicilin proniká do CNS jen při meningitidě.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="996.7" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1002.7" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1001.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Placenta</text><text x="294.0" y="1001.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Placentární bariéra je PROPUSTNĚJŠÍ, než se obvykle čeká — většina</text><text x="294.0" y="1022.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">léčiv jí projde.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1049.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1055.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1054.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Anestetikum v zánětu</text><text x="294.0" y="1054.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">V kyselém zánětlivém prostředí převáží nabitá forma lokálního</text><text x="294.0" y="1074.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">anestetika, která neprojde k sodíkovému kanálu — proto tam</text><text x="294.0" y="1095.0" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nezabírá.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1121.4" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M30.7,1118.9 L869.3,1118.1 Q871.7,1120.3 870.6,1127.8 L871.1,1178.0 Q872.6,1188.2 869.4,1187.2 L32.0,1187.1 Q24.5,1187.7 25.5,1176.9 L23.2,1124.8 Q24.4,1119.8 30.0,1121.3" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M33.8,1122.7 L865.3,1118.7 Q874.7,1121.8 874.7,1124.5 L876.5,1175.6 Q874.8,1183.2 865.2,1183.2 L29.7,1183.3 Q28.5,1188.0 23.4,1180.9 L26.5,1127.5 Q23.0,1120.0 35.1,1120.6" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1142.4" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1142.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Krev–varle a krev–sítnice jsou další bariéry; naopak zánět propustnost bariér obecně zvyšuje, což</text><text x="66.0" y="1161.6" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">mění dávkování.</text></svg>

text-anchor="middle">6</text><ellipse cx="664.6" cy="137.4" rx="22.6" ry="6.0" fill="#23303A" opacity="0.12"/><text x="664.6" y="139.8" font-size="59.51178176647053" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="middle">½</text><circle cx="688.4" cy="99.3" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="688.4" y="105.3" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="723.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">První stanice A</text><text x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ABSORPCE — vstup do krve; její rozsah vyjadřuje biologická</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">dostupnost F (i.v. = 100 %).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="770.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="776.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Druhá stanice D</text><text x="294.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">DISTRIBUCE — rozvod do tkání; popisuje ji distribuční objem Vd</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(poměr dávky k plazmatické koncentraci).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="823.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="829.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="828.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Třetí stanice M</text><text x="294.0" y="828.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">METABOLISMUS — přeměna, hlavně v játrech; I. fáze (oxidace,</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">redukce, hydrolýza) a II. fáze (konjugace).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="875.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="881.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="880.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Čtvrtá stanice E</text><text x="294.0" y="880.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">EXKRECE — vyloučení, hlavně ledvinami; dále žlučí, plícemi, mlékem.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="908.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="914.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="913.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Spojka M + E</text><text x="294.0" y="913.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ELIMINACE = metabolismus PLUS exkrece. Není totéž co vylučování —</text><text x="294.0" y="933.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">to je jen její část.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="960.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="966.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="965.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Vodárna</text><text x="294.0" y="965.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">CLEARANCE — objem krve zcela očištěný za jednotku času; sčítá se</text><text x="294.0" y="986.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">renální a jaterní.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1013.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1019.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1018.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Půlená cedule</text><text x="294.0" y="1018.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Biologický poločas — doba poklesu na polovinu. Ustálený stav</text><text x="294.0" y="1038.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nastane za 4–5 poločasů a stejně dlouho trvá vymizení.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1065.1" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M29.3,1062.0 L868.6,1065.0 Q874.3,1066.7 874.3,1067.0 L871.3,1120.1 Q876.8,1130.8 868.9,1132.0 L28.7,1125.8 Q27.5,1129.1 25.1,1123.6 L24.1,1069.5 Q23.2,1063.0 29.0,1062.4" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M32.3,1064.2 L867.2,1062.1 Q876.4,1062.4 876.3,1068.1 L876.3,1120.9 Q875.0,1131.5 865.1,1130.5 L33.2,1130.7 Q26.5,1130.5 29.0,1123.2 L26.9,1070.4 Q27.3,1066.0 32.9,1066.0" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1086.1" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1086.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Vysoké Vd znamená, že látka sedí ve tkáních, ne v krvi — proto ji hemodialýza neodstraní (digoxin,</text><text x="66.0" y="1105.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">antidepresiva).</text></svg>

PRVNÍHO ŘÁDU — odbourává se konstantní PODÍL za čas</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(polovina za poločas). Platí pro naprostou většinu léčiv, protože</text><text x="294.0" y="763.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">enzymy mají kapacitní rezervu.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="790.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="796.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Přesýpací hodiny</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">KINETIKA NULTÉHO ŘÁDU — enzym je nasycený, odbourává konstantní</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">MNOŽSTVÍ za čas bez ohledu na koncentraci. Pokles je lineární a</text><text x="294.0" y="836.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">poločas ztrácí smysl.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="863.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="869.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Korbel piva</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Ethanol se odbourává řádově 0,1–0,15 ‰ za hodinu, vždy stejně —</text><text x="294.0" y="889.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">proto NELZE počítat „za dva poločasy bude polovina“ a rychlost</text><text x="294.0" y="909.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nejde urychlit.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="936.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="942.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="941.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Výhybka</text><text x="294.0" y="941.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Michaelisova–Mentenové (saturační) kinetika je přechod mezi obojím:</text><text x="294.0" y="962.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">při nízké koncentraci první řád, po nasycení enzymu nultý.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="989.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="995.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Přetékající hrnec</text><text x="294.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">U saturačního léčiva stačí malé zvýšení dávky a hladina vyskočí do</text><text x="294.0" y="1014.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">toxického pásma — fenytoin (nystagmus, ataxie, zmatenost),</text><text x="294.0" y="1035.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">salicyláty, theofylin. Proto se měří hladiny.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1062.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1068.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1067.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Čtyři zářezy</text><text x="294.0" y="1067.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Ustálený stav nastane za 4–5 poločasů a stejně dlouho trvá vymizení</text><text x="294.0" y="1087.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">po vysazení.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1115.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1121.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1120.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Nasycovací dávka</text><text x="294.0" y="1120.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">U dlouhého poločasu se čekání zkracuje nasycovací dávkou.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1146.5" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M30.6,1142.8 L868.8,1144.0 Q871.8,1143.3 873.3,1153.5 L870.6,1203.0 Q875.0,1207.2 864.9,1209.4 L32.3,1212.7 Q28.9,1210.3 25.7,1203.6 L26.4,1149.3 Q25.1,1148.9 30.2,1144.6" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M34.4,1143.8 L865.2,1147.7 Q874.2,1142.8 873.6,1150.3 L872.0,1205.1 Q871.5,1212.6 869.2,1208.2 L32.0,1207.5 Q26.0,1213.1 22.8,1203.6 L26.4,1153.6 Q24.6,1143.0 33.9,1146.6" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1167.5" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1167.5" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Poločas má smysl jen u kinetiky prvního řádu — u nultého řádu se mění s koncentrací, a proto se</text><text x="66.0" y="1186.7" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">neuvádí.</text></svg>

font-size="13.5" font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">CO JE VE SCÉNĚ</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="692.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="698.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Vyrovnané váhy</text><text x="294.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Cmax není konec vstřebávání — je to okamžik, kdy se rychlost vstupu</text><text x="294.0" y="718.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">vyrovná rychlosti eliminace. Vstřebávání pokračuje i po něm, jen ho</text><text x="294.0" y="738.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">eliminace převáží.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="765.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="771.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Křivka přes kopec</text><text x="294.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">BATEMANOVA FUNKCE popisuje průběh koncentrace po jednorázovém</text><text x="294.0" y="791.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">perorálním podání: vzestupná fáze (převažuje absorpce), vrchol,</text><text x="294.0" y="811.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">sestupná fáze (převažuje eliminace).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="838.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="844.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="843.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Počítadlo plochy</text><text x="294.0" y="843.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">AUC — plocha pod křivkou = celková expozice organismu; podle ní se</text><text x="294.0" y="864.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">srovnává originál s generikem (bioekvivalence).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="891.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="897.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="896.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Vrátnice s výběrčím</text><text x="294.0" y="896.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Biologická dostupnost F = podíl dávky, který se dostane NEZMĚNĚNÝ</text><text x="294.0" y="916.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">do systémového oběhu; snižuje ji neúplné vstřebání a first-pass</text><text x="294.0" y="937.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">metabolismus.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="964.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="970.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="969.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Stopky</text><text x="294.0" y="969.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">tmax vypovídá o RYCHLOSTI vstřebávání, Cmax a AUC o jeho ROZSAHU.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="996.7" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1002.7" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1001.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Sklenice mléka</text><text x="294.0" y="1001.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Vstřebání ovlivní léková forma, rozpustnost, pH, jídlo, motilita a</text><text x="294.0" y="1022.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">prokrvení; antacida, železo a vápník váží tetracykliny a chinolony.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1049.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1055.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1054.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Dvě shodné křivky</text><text x="294.0" y="1054.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Generikum musí mít shodné AUC i Cmax v předepsaném rozmezí — proto</text><text x="294.0" y="1074.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">se u něj nezkoumá znovu účinnost.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1101.0" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M29.2,1096.7 L868.1,1101.7 Q874.4,1100.4 877.0,1103.6 L870.8,1156.0 Q872.5,1167.4 871.2,1165.1 L29.1,1162.3 Q25.1,1162.5 25.3,1161.1 L27.3,1104.8 Q23.9,1103.0 29.2,1103.4" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M33.0,1102.9 L867.2,1102.0 Q875.9,1101.1 875.2,1104.7 L871.8,1161.2 Q871.7,1165.0 869.1,1166.4 L28.7,1165.8 Q23.9,1166.5 28.4,1156.6 L26.8,1107.4 Q28.8,1099.9 31.7,1102.9" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1122.0" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1122.0" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Vyšší Cmax při stejném AUC znamená rychlejší, ale krátkodobější účinek — proto retardovaná forma</text><text x="66.0" y="1141.3" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">snižuje Cmax, aniž by měnila celkovou expozici.</text></svg>
<div style="break-after:page"></div>

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 1243" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block" font-family="Liberation Sans, Carlito, Calibri, Arial, sans-serif"><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="1243" fill="#FFFDF7"/><text x="26.0" y="48.0" font-size="34" font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">O14</text><text x="112.0" y="44.0" font-size="21" font-weight="700" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Distribuce, distribuční objem, redistribuce, vazba na bílkoviny,</text><text x="112.0" y="71.7" font-size="21" font-weight="700" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">bariéry</text><path d="M24.4,98.9 L875.3,100.1 Q875.1,98.6 873.9,99.5 L874.1,98.7 Q875.2,99.1 873.8,99.6 L26.6,100.4 Q25.1,99.3 26.4,98.4 L26.9,99.1 Q26.2,99.4 27.4,99.2" fill="none" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M25.4,100.7 L874.2,100.6 Q874.8,99.8 873.3,99.3 L872.9,99.0 Q874.2,100.0 872.6,99.4 L26.1,99.2 Q26.3,98.2 25.6,101.0 L25.0,98.7 Q27.1,100.8 25.2,100.1" fill="none" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><rect x="27.0" y="116.4" width="846.0" height="76.2" rx="10" fill="#FDE9A8" opacity="0.5"/><path d="M30.1,116.1 L867.8,117.2 Q877.3,113.5 877.0,122.5 L873.8,187.9 Q873.2,192.8 866.9,193.7 L30.1,190.4 Q24.5,194.7 23.3,188.1 L25.3,124.3 Q24.5,113.7 33.2,112.9" fill="none" stroke="#8A6A18" stroke-width="2.1" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M33.3,118.1 L867.9,116.4 Q871.9,115.3 871.3,123.7 L873.9,190.2 Q876.9,193.0 868.4,191.6 L30.4,194.7 Q28.8,190.3 26.9,188.1 L26.2,120.5 Q24.7,115.9 33.7,114.1" fill="none" stroke="#8A6A18" stroke-width="2.1" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="46.0" y="140.4" font-size="19" font-weight="700" fill="#4A3A08" text-anchor="start">SKLAD S VELKOU NÁDOBOU — Vd je ZDÁNLIVÝ objem, ne skutečný. font-size="13.5" font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">CO JE VE SCÉNĚ</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="745.6" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="751.6" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="750.6" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Obří nádoba</text><text x="294.0" y="750.6" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Vd = dávka ÷ plazmatická koncentrace. Je to počítaná veličina, ne</text><text x="294.0" y="770.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">anatomický prostor — může mnohonásobně převýšit objem těla.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="798.3" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="804.3" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="803.3" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Gauč ve skladu</text><text x="294.0" y="803.3" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Vysoké Vd = lék uložený ve tkáních (tuk, sval), v krvi ho je málo —</text><text x="294.0" y="823.6" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">proto ho HEMODIALÝZA NEODSTRANÍ (digoxin, antidepresiva).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="850.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="856.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="855.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Řetěz k albuminu</text><text x="294.0" y="855.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Účinná je jen VOLNÁ frakce. Albumin váže kyselé látky (warfarin,</text><text x="294.0" y="876.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">fenytoin), OROSOMUKOID (kyselý α1-glykoprotein) zásadité. Při</text><text x="294.0" y="896.6" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">hypoalbuminemii stoupá volná frakce a s ní účinek i toxicita.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="923.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="929.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="928.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Brána do mozku</text><text x="294.0" y="928.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Hematoencefalická bariéra — těsné spoje a P-glykoprotein; propustí</text><text x="294.0" y="949.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">jen lipofilní a nenabité látky.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="976.6" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="982.6" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="981.6" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Placenta</text><text x="294.0" y="981.6" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Placentární bariéra je propustnější, než se čeká — proto se u</text><text x="294.0" y="1001.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">gravidity vždy ptáme, co projde k plodu.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1029.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1035.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1034.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Usínající pacient</text><text x="294.0" y="1034.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">REDISTRIBUCE: thiopental uspí rychle (jde do mozku) a probudí</text><text x="294.0" y="1054.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">rychle (přesune se do svalu a tuku) — konec účinku dělá PŘESUN, ne</text><text x="294.0" y="1074.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">eliminace.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1102.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1108.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1107.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Vytěsnění z vazby</text><text x="294.0" y="1107.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Sulfonamidy vytěsní warfarin z albuminu; samo o sobě bývá klinicky</text><text x="294.0" y="1127.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">přechodné — nebezpečné je, když se přidá i útlum metabolismu.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1153.8" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M34.8,1151.1 L865.3,1155.7 Q876.9,1155.0 871.5,1159.2 L873.3,1214.2 Q870.7,1220.4 869.4,1220.8 L30.7,1215.7 Q29.1,1215.1 24.5,1214.3 L26.3,1158.3 Q23.9,1155.3 33.9,1155.5" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M34.9,1151.9 L864.7,1150.8 Q873.2,1149.5 874.7,1162.2 L874.5,1209.2 Q873.9,1218.5 864.7,1214.6 L31.8,1218.0 Q24.4,1214.8 29.0,1212.6 L23.2,1158.3 Q25.7,1153.0 30.6,1152.5" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1174.8" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1174.8" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Hydrofilní látky mají nízké Vd (zůstávají v krvi a extracelulárně), lipofilní vysoké — proto se u</text><text x="66.0" y="1194.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">obézního pacienta liší dávkování podle typu léčiva.</text></svg>
