polovinou</text><text x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Biologický poločas t½ — doba, za kterou klesne koncentrace na</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">polovinu. U kinetiky prvního řádu je konstantní a nezávisí na</text><text x="294.0" y="763.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">dávce.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="790.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="796.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Strmý spád α</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">FÁZE α — po i.v. podání klesá koncentrace nejprve strmě, protože se</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">látka rozvádí do tkání (distribuce).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="843.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="849.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Pozvolný spád β</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">FÁZE β — pozvolný pokles odpovídá skutečné eliminaci; poločas se</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">počítá právě z ní.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="896.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="902.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Kohoutek clearance</text><text x="294.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">CL = objem plazmy zcela očištěný za jednotku času; CL = CLren +</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">CLhep. Vztah: t½ = 0,693 × Vd ÷ CL.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="948.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="954.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Čtyři zářezy</text><text x="294.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Za 4–5 poločasů se dosáhne ustáleného stavu — a stejně dlouho trvá</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">vymizení po vysazení.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1001.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1007.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Konstanta kel</text><text x="294.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Eliminační konstanta kel — podíl látky odstraněný za jednotku času;</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">t½ = 0,693 ÷ kel.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1054.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1060.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1059.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Ledvina a játra</text><text x="294.0" y="1059.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Poločas roste, když klesne clearance (jaterní nebo renální selhání)</text><text x="294.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">NEBO když stoupne Vd — proto nestačí sledovat jen ledviny.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1105.8" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M29.1,1107.0 L867.6,1107.4 Q874.6,1103.0 872.8,1108.9 L877.1,1160.8 Q874.9,1166.1 866.7,1167.0 L29.4,1167.6 Q22.8,1171.5 25.5,1160.6 L23.6,1108.9 Q24.6,1104.9 font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">CO JE VE SCÉNĚ</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="692.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="698.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Plnící se sud</text><text x="294.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">KUMULACE nastane, je-li dávkovací interval kratší než doba potřebná</text><text x="294.0" y="718.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">k eliminaci; hladina se ustálí po 4–5 poločasech.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="745.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="751.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="750.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Startovací raketa</text><text x="294.0" y="750.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">NASYCOVACÍ dávka = Vd × cílová koncentrace. Podává se, když je</text><text x="294.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">poločas dlouhý a nelze čekat 4–5 poločasů na účinek.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="798.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="804.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="803.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Doplňovací konev</text><text x="294.0" y="803.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">UDRŽOVACÍ dávka = CL × cílová koncentrace × interval. Nahrazuje to,</text><text x="294.0" y="823.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">co se za interval vyloučí.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="850.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="856.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="855.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Plochá vlnovka</text><text x="294.0" y="855.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Krátký interval a malé dávky = plochá, stabilní hladina. Dlouhý</text><text x="294.0" y="876.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">interval a velké dávky = větší kolísání mezi vrcholem a údolím.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="903.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="909.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="908.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Přetékající okraj</text><text x="294.0" y="908.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Kolísání je nebezpečné u léčiv s úzkým terapeutickým oknem — proto</text><text x="294.0" y="928.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">se u nich volí kratší interval.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="956.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="962.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="961.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Kumulační index</text><text x="294.0" y="961.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Poměr koncentrace v ustáleném stavu k té po první dávce; udává,</text><text x="294.0" y="981.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">kolikrát se hladina nahromadí.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1008.7" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1014.7" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1013.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Zúžený odtok</text><text x="294.0" y="1013.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Při renální insuficienci se krátí UDRŽOVACÍ dávka (závisí na</text><text x="294.0" y="1034.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">clearance), ale nasycovací zůstává stejná (závisí na Vd).</text><rect x="27.0" y="1060.4" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M30.3,1061.0 L867.8,1059.8 Q872.0,1059.7 871.8,1068.7 L876.4,1116.9 Q873.1,1122.7 869.5,1124.5 L33.0,1121.0 Q24.1,1126.0 26.1,1120.7 L25.9,1062.0 Q26.5,1057.2 30.6,1061.9" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M28.9,1056.6 L865.1,1062.8 Q873.3,1062.4 871.5,1065.7 L876.6,1121.0 Q871.7,1121.7 870.4,1122.0 L29.8,1126.6 Q27.7,1127.2 28.6,1119.4 L26.2,1062.9 Q27.6,1059.6 31.4,1062.4" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1081.4" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1081.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Nasycovací dávka se řídí distribučním objemem, udržovací clearance — proto se u renálního selhání</text><text x="66.0" y="1100.7" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">mění jen jedna z nich.</text></svg>

fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="723.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Kladivo I. fáze</text><text x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">I. FÁZE — oxidace (hlavně cytochromy P450, nejvíc CYP3A4), redukce,</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">hydrolýza. Vzniká reaktivnější metabolit s odhalenou funkční</text><text x="294.0" y="763.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">skupinou.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="790.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="796.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Štítkovač II. fáze</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">II. FÁZE — konjugace: glukuronidace, sulfatace, acetylace,</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">methylace, vazba na glutathion. Produkt je velký, polární a snadno</text><text x="294.0" y="836.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">vyloučitelný.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="863.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="869.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Kapka vody</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Smyslem je změnit lipofilní látku na hydrofilní — jinak by se v</text><text x="294.0" y="889.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ledvinách stále zpětně vstřebávala.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="916.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="922.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Jiskra aktivace</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">PROLÉČIVO se aktivuje až metabolismem: kodein → morfin (CYP2D6),</text><text x="294.0" y="941.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">enalapril → enalaprilát, cyklofosfamid, klopidogrel.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="969.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="975.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Toxický metabolit</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Metabolismus může vytvořit i jed: paracetamol → NAPQI, methanol →</text><text x="294.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">kyselina mravenčí („letální syntéza“).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1021.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1027.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Novorozenec</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Nezralá glukuronidace u novorozence — odtud gray baby syndrom po</text><text x="294.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">chloramfenikolu.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1074.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1080.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Jiná pracoviště</text><text x="294.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Kromě jater i střevní stěna, plíce, ledviny a plazma (esterázy). U</text><text x="294.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">starších klesá hlavně I. fáze, II. zůstává poměrně zachovaná.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1126.1" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M34.6,1126.4 L865.2,1126.2 Q873.8,1124.7 871.5,1132.8 L873.7,1181.2 Q876.2,1187.5 869.4,1187.7 L29.1,1192.7 Q25.8,1191.3 23.0,1184.6 L25.1,1127.8 Q26.7,1128.0 33.4,1123.9" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M34.6,1122.5 L871.3,1122.7 Q875.1,1126.8 871.8,1130.0 L875.2,1183.4 Q873.7,1188.6 868.8,1187.2 L31.3,1187.0 Q23.6,1189.5 28.2,1185.3 L25.0,1134.2 Q27.0,1125.0 31.1,1125.2" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1147.1" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1147.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Metabolit není vždy neúčinný — může být účinnější než původní látka, stejně toxický, nebo teprve</text><text x="66.0" y="1166.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">toxický. Proto se sleduje osud metabolitu, ne jen léčiva.</text></svg>

cy="717.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="723.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Celnice u brány</text><text x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Vše vstřebané ze střeva projde portální žílou JÁTRY dřív, než se</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">dostane do systémového oběhu — first-pass efekt.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="770.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="776.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Zmenšená zásilka</text><text x="294.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Silný first-pass mají nitroglycerin, propranolol, morfin,</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">verapamil, lidokain — proto se nitroglycerin podává sublingválně a</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">lidokain se ústy nepodává vůbec.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="843.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="849.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Kruhová cesta</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ENTEROHEPATÁLNÍ OBĚH: látka se vyloučí žlučí, ve střevě se uvolní a</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">znovu vstřebá — prodlužuje účinek (kontraceptiva; proto mohou</text><text x="294.0" y="889.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">antibiotika jejich účinnost snížit).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="916.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="922.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Pumpa u brány</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">U léčiv s VYSOKOU jaterní extrakcí limituje eliminaci PRŮTOK játry</text><text x="294.0" y="941.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">— při srdečním selhání a šoku hladiny stoupají.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="969.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="975.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Enzymová dílna</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">U NÍZKÉ extrakce rozhoduje enzymatická kapacita a vazba na</text><text x="294.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">bílkoviny, nikoli průtok.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1021.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1027.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Objízdná trasa</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Játra obcházejí sublingvální, bukální, transdermální a parenterální</text><text x="294.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">podání; rektální jen zčásti.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1074.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1080.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Nízký albumin</text><text x="294.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Při jaterním selhání klesá metabolismus i tvorba albuminu, roste</text><text x="294.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">volná frakce a zkratový oběh obchází játra — dávky se snižují.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1126.1" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M33.5,1122.2 L867.3,1127.3 Q876.3,1127.3 871.6,1131.7 L873.8,1183.1 Q874.7,1189.2 867.1,1192.9 L31.7,1188.9 Q25.5,1190.4 24.2,1184.1 L25.3,1132.4 Q24.1,1123.3 32.4,1122.2" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M33.9,1124.1 L869.5,1124.6 Q871.1,1122.9 875.4,1128.8 L876.5,1183.3 Q874.4,1187.2 871.0,1192.5 L33.2,1191.5 Q24.8,1190.4 23.5,1183.7 L23.0,1131.7 Q24.8,1124.1 35.3,1126.2" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1147.1" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1147.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Perorální dávka bývá mnohonásobně vyšší než nitrožilní právě kvůli first-pass efektu — záměna cest</text><text x="66.0" y="1166.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">podání proto může znamenat předávkování.</text></svg>

font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">CO JE VE SCÉNĚ</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="692.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="698.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Spadlá závora</text><text x="294.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">INHIBICE je RYCHLÁ (hodiny až dny): hladina druhého léčiva stoupá a</text><text x="294.0" y="718.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">hrozí TOXICITA.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="745.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="751.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="750.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Rostoucí továrna</text><text x="294.0" y="750.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">INDUKCE je POMALÁ (dny až týdny, protože se musí nasyntetizovat</text><text x="294.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nový enzym) a stejně dlouho odeznívá: hladina klesá a LÉČBA SELŽE.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="798.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="804.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="803.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Grapefruit</text><text x="294.0" y="803.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Inhibitory: grapefruitová šťáva, azolová antimykotika, makrolidy</text><text x="294.0" y="823.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(erythromycin, klarithromycin — nikoli azithromycin),</text><text x="294.0" y="843.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ciprofloxacin, verapamil, amiodaron, ritonavir.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="871.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="877.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="876.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Oheň pod kotlem</text><text x="294.0" y="876.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Induktory: rifampicin, karbamazepin, fenytoin, fenobarbital,</text><text x="294.0" y="896.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">třezalka tečkovaná; chronicky alkohol a kouření (CYP1A2).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="923.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="929.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="928.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Selhávající antikoncepce</text><text x="294.0" y="928.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Rifampicin sníží účinnost hormonální kontracepce, warfarinu a</text><text x="294.0" y="949.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">antiretrovirotik.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="976.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="982.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="981.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Bolavý sval</text><text x="294.0" y="981.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Klarithromycin se statinem zvýší riziko rabdomyolýzy.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1008.7" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1014.7" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1013.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Obrácená šipka</text><text x="294.0" y="1013.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">U PROLÉČIVA je to obráceně: inhibitor CYP2D6 (fluoxetin, paroxetin)</text><text x="294.0" y="1034.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">SNÍŽÍ účinek kodeinu a klopidogrelu, protože zabrání jejich</text><text x="294.0" y="1054.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">aktivaci.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1080.7" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M30.5,1077.1 L869.6,1077.1 Q876.9,1079.2 871.6,1084.1 L877.3,1140.9 Q874.8,1147.1 864.8,1142.2 L29.6,1144.4 Q24.0,1147.4 22.8,1138.0 L24.6,1083.3 Q26.8,1080.5 29.3,1079.1" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M34.1,1077.1 L866.9,1078.0 Q877.4,1078.5 872.1,1085.1 L872.8,1135.5 Q873.9,1144.7 865.1,1141.2 L32.0,1145.6 Q23.7,1142.3 28.9,1135.9 L24.9,1085.9 Q26.5,1082.4 30.1,1080.6" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1101.7" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1101.7" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Interakce nemizí okamžitě po vysazení induktoru — enzym se odbourává týdny, takže hladina druhého</text><text x="66.0" y="1121.0" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">léčiva pak může naopak vyskočit.</text></svg>

FILTRACE — projde jen VOLNÁ (nenavázaná) frakce; látka</text><text x="294.0" y="718.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">vázaná na albumin se nefiltruje.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="745.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="751.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="750.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Boční přítok</text><text x="294.0" y="750.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">TUBULÁRNÍ SEKRECE je aktivní transport se společnými přenašeči —</text><text x="294.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">proto probenecid zpomaluje vylučování penicilinu (soupeří o týž</text><text x="294.0" y="791.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">přenašeč).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="818.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="824.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="823.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Zpětné nasávání</text><text x="294.0" y="823.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ZPĚTNÁ RESORPCE: lipofilní a nenabité látky se z tubulu vstřebávají</text><text x="294.0" y="843.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">zpět. Alkalizace moči bikarbonátem urychlí vyloučení kyselých látek</text><text x="294.0" y="864.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(salicyláty, barbituráty).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="891.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="897.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="896.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Výdech</text><text x="294.0" y="896.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Extrarenálně: plícemi (inhalační anestetika, ethanol — odtud</text><text x="294.0" y="916.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">dechová zkouška).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="944.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="950.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="949.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Žlučovod</text><text x="294.0" y="949.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Žlučí a stolicí, s možností enterohepatálního oběhu.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="976.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="982.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="981.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Mateřské mléko</text><text x="294.0" y="981.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Do mléka přechází nejspíš látka lipofilní, málo vázaná na bílkoviny</text><text x="294.0" y="1001.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">a zásaditá — proto se u kojící ptáme na každý lék.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1029.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1035.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1034.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Zúžená ledvina</text><text x="294.0" y="1034.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Dávky léčiv vylučovaných ledvinami se snižují podle clearance</text><text x="294.0" y="1054.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">kreatininu — aminoglykosidy, digoxin, metformin, lithium, mnohá</text><text x="294.0" y="1074.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">antibiotika.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1101.0" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M31.6,1101.1 L869.4,1102.0 Q875.4,1098.1 876.8,1106.3 L873.4,1161.1 Q874.1,1162.0 869.1,1161.3 L31.3,1166.7 Q26.4,1165.2 24.4,1156.7 L24.7,1108.0 Q23.0,1100.1 30.7,1099.6" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M31.0,1097.2 L870.3,1096.7 Q875.2,1096.8 876.4,1109.1 L873.6,1161.8 Q876.2,1167.3 867.6,1166.2 L33.4,1166.3 Q28.2,1161.7 23.4,1155.8 L23.2,1105.9 Q28.6,1101.6 30.9,1102.3" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1122.0" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1122.0" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Vyloučit se dá jen látka vodorozpustná — proto biotransformace předchází exkreci; lipofilní látka by</text><text x="66.0" y="1141.3" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">se v tubulu jen stále vstřebávala zpět.</text></svg>

vazba na cílovou strukturu: receptor, enzym,</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">iontový kanál, přenašeč nebo nukleovou kyselinu. Je strukturně</text><text x="294.0" y="763.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">závislý a nasytitelný.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="790.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="796.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Hromada soli</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">NESPECIFICKÝ účinek — fyzikálně-chemický, bez cílové struktury:</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">antacida neutralizují kyselinu, osmotická projímadla táhnou vodu,</text><text x="294.0" y="836.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">aktivní uhlí adsorbuje.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="863.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="869.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Klíč otvírá zámek</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">AGONISTA se váže a receptor AKTIVUJE (má afinitu i vnitřní</text><text x="294.0" y="889.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">aktivitu).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="916.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="922.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Klíč v zámku bez otočení</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ANTAGONISTA se váže, ale neaktivuje (má jen afinitu) — brání</text><text x="294.0" y="941.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">navázání agonisty.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="969.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="975.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Enzym pod lupou</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Enzymy jako cíl: statiny na HMG-CoA-reduktázu, ACE inhibitory,</text><text x="294.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">inhibitory protonové pumpy.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1021.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1027.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Iontový kanál</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Kanály a přenašeče: lokální anestetika na sodíkové kanály,</text><text x="294.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">blokátory kalciových kanálů, SSRI na serotoninový transportér.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1074.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1080.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Šroubovice DNA</text><text x="294.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Nukleové kyseliny jako cíl: cytostatika, chinolony (topoizomeráza),</text><text x="294.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">rifampicin (RNA-polymeráza).</text><rect x="27.0" y="1126.1" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M28.7,1123.6 L866.1,1123.6 font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Magnet</text><text x="294.0" y="725.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">AFINITA — jak pevně se ligand váže; určuje potřebnou koncentraci.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="752.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="758.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="757.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Blesk</text><text x="294.0" y="757.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">VNITŘNÍ AKTIVITA — co se po navázání stane. Plný agonista 1,</text><text x="294.0" y="778.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">parciální mezi 0 a 1 (buprenorfin), antagonista 0, inverzní</text><text x="294.0" y="798.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">agonista pod 0 (H1-antihistaminika).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="825.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="831.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="830.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Rychlé dveře</text><text x="294.0" y="830.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">IONOTROPNÍ receptory jsou samy iontovým kanálem — účinek za</text><text x="294.0" y="851.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">MILISEKUNDY: nikotinový acetylcholinový, GABA-A, NMDA.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="878.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="884.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="883.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Telefon s ústřednou</text><text x="294.0" y="883.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">METABOTROPNÍ receptory jdou přes G-protein a druhého posla — účinek</text><text x="294.0" y="903.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">za SEKUNDY: muskarinové, adrenergní, opioidní.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="931.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="937.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="936.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Jádro buňky</text><text x="294.0" y="936.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">NITROBUNĚČNÉ (jaderné) receptory mění přepis genů — účinek za</text><text x="294.0" y="956.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">HODINY až DNY: kortikosteroidy, hormony štítné žlázy, pohlavní</text><text x="294.0" y="976.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">hormony.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1004.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1010.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1009.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Přetlačovaná o zámek</text><text x="294.0" y="1009.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">KOMPETITIVNÍ antagonismus je překonatelný vyšší dávkou agonisty</text><text x="294.0" y="1029.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(posun křivky doprava); NEKOMPETITIVNÍ nepřekonatelný (snižuje</text><text x="294.0" y="1049.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">maximum).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1077.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1083.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1082.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Ubývající zámky</text><text x="294.0" y="1082.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">DOWN-REGULACE při trvalé stimulaci vysvětluje toleranci;</text><text x="294.0" y="1102.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">UP-REGULACE při dlouhé blokádě vysvětluje rebound po náhlém</text><text x="294.0" y="1122.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">vysazení β-blokátoru.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1149.1" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M32.2,1150.9 L869.4,1149.6 Q875.7,1150.2 871.8,1154.9 L874.3,1209.5 opacity="0.8"/><path d="M32.9,1147.0 L867.6,1149.1 Q875.7,1147.0 876.0,1152.9 L871.8,1208.1 Q874.8,1213.4 870.5,1214.3 L34.6,1215.7 Q25.2,1209.9 24.8,1205.5 L22.9,1155.8 Q23.8,1145.9 33.2,1150.6" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1170.1" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1170.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Pomalý nástup kortikoidů není vlastnost lékové formy, ale mechanismu — musí proběhnout přepis genů a</text><text x="66.0" y="1189.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">syntéza bílkovin.</text></svg>

okno</text><text x="294.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">TERAPEUTICKÉ OKNO — rozmezí koncentrací mezi minimální účinnou a</text><text x="294.0" y="718.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">toxickou.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="745.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="751.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="750.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Pravítko</text><text x="294.0" y="750.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">TERAPEUTICKÝ INDEX TI = TD50 ÷ ED50 (u zvířat LD50 ÷ ED50). ED50 =</text><text x="294.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">dávka účinná u poloviny, TD50 = toxická u poloviny, LD50 = smrtelná</text><text x="294.0" y="791.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">pro polovinu.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="818.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="824.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="823.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Fonendoskop a odběr</text><text x="294.0" y="823.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Úzký index mají digoxin, warfarin, theofylin, lithium, fenytoin,</text><text x="294.0" y="843.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">aminoglykosidy, cyklosporin — u nich se MĚŘÍ plazmatické hladiny.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="871.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="877.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="876.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Číslice NNT</text><text x="294.0" y="876.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">NNT — kolik pacientů je třeba léčit, aby se u jednoho zabránilo</text><text x="294.0" y="896.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">příhodě; nízké NNT = účinná léčba. Obdobně NNH pro poškození.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="923.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="929.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="928.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Esovitá křivka</text><text x="294.0" y="928.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Vztah dávky a účinku je sigmoidní (v logaritmickém měřítku).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="956.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="962.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="961.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Silák</text><text x="294.0" y="961.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ÚČINNOST (efficacy) = jak vysokého maxima lék dosáhne.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="988.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="994.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="993.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Malá tableta</text><text x="294.0" y="993.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">POTENCE = jak malá dávka k tomu stačí. Potentnější lék NENÍ lepší</text><text x="294.0" y="1013.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">lék — rozhoduje maximální účinek a bezpečnost.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1040.1" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M35.4,1036.3 L865.6,1038.4 Q875.0,1042.3 876.6,1045.7 L875.1,1094.9 Q871.0,1103.4 869.2,1103.6 L30.8,1104.2 Q26.8,1102.6 26.4,1094.5 L22.6,1047.6 Q27.4,1041.7 30.1,1040.4" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M29.4,1042.4 L866.8,1040.4 Q873.5,1040.6 876.8,1047.4 L876.5,1095.0 Q875.6,1105.5 868.8,1105.1 L33.2,1100.7 Q24.9,1105.9 25.8,1096.9 L26.2,1044.9 Q22.8,1042.2 32.4,1039.6" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1061.1" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1061.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Riziko se vždy váží proti prospěchu: u onkologické léčby se přijímá terapeutický index, který by u</text><text x="66.0" y="1080.3" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">léku na rýmu byl nepřijatelný.</text></svg>

font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">CO JE VE SCÉNĚ</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="692.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="698.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Novorozenec</text><text x="294.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Nezralá glukuronidace, vyšší podíl tělesné vody, nižší vazba na</text><text x="294.0" y="718.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">bílkoviny a nezralá glomerulární filtrace.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="745.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="751.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="750.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Seniorka</text><text x="294.0" y="750.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Nižší glomerulární filtrace, nižší albumin, méně svaloviny a více</text><text x="294.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">tuku (lipofilní léčiva se hromadí), vyšší citlivost CNS.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="798.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="804.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="803.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Ledvina a játra</text><text x="294.0" y="803.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Jaterní selhání snižuje metabolismus a tvorbu albuminu; renální</text><text x="294.0" y="823.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">insuficience zpomaluje vylučování. U obojího se dávky snižují.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="850.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="856.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="855.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Šroubovice</text><text x="294.0" y="855.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Polymorfismus CYP2D6, CYP2C9, CYP2C19, NAT2, TPMT rozhoduje, zda je</text><text x="294.0" y="876.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">člověk pomalý, nebo ultrarychlý metabolizátor.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="903.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="909.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="908.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Talíř s jídlem</text><text x="294.0" y="908.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Jídlo mění vstřebávání (mléko a antacida váží tetracykliny),</text><text x="294.0" y="928.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">grapefruit inhibuje CYP3A4, kouření indukuje CYP1A2 (theofylin,</text><text x="294.0" y="949.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">klozapin, olanzapin).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="976.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="982.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="981.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Těhotná</text><text x="294.0" y="981.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Roste objem distribuce a glomerulární filtrace, klesá albumin — a</text><text x="294.0" y="1001.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">přibývá otázka, co projde placentou.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1029.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1035.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1034.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Změněná citlivost</text><text x="294.0" y="1034.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Mění se i DYNAMIKA: u seniorů vyšší citlivost na benzodiazepiny a</text><text x="294.0" y="1054.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">opioidy, při hypokalemii vyšší toxicita digoxinu.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1080.7" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M34.9,1078.5 L867.0,1077.5 Q872.0,1081.8 871.8,1083.2 L871.2,1139.4 Q877.2,1145.3 867.0,1147.3 L33.4,1141.6 Q26.9,1142.7 23.8,1135.0 L23.9,1082.8 Q24.5,1078.3 33.5,1078.7" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M32.4,1078.6 L870.0,1081.5 Q871.2,1082.6 876.4,1085.1 L876.2,1137.2 Q875.0,1146.6 866.9,1145.3 L35.3,1144.6 Q25.4,1146.6 25.5,1139.9 L22.8,1084.6 Q23.4,1080.3 32.1,1083.0" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1101.7" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1101.7" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Kinetika i dynamika se mění současně — proto nestačí přepočítat dávku podle hmotnosti, ale je nutné</text><text x="66.0" y="1121.0" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">počítat i s jinou odpovědí cílové struktury.</text></svg>

ry="6.8" fill="#23303A" opacity="0.12"/><text x="683.3" y="153.3" font-size="68.01705556418747" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="middle">🛒</text><circle cx="710.5" cy="107.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="710.5" y="113.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text></g><path d="M32.9,157.4 L867.7,159.1 Q872.8,157.1 873.1,166.5 L876.2,620.1 Q874.2,627.6 866.1,626.5 L33.5,629.8 Q27.6,627.9 27.4,622.8 L28.2,167.2 Q23.6,156.8 35.5,158.9" fill="none" stroke="#23303A" stroke-width="2.4" font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">CO JE VE SCÉNĚ</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="692.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="698.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Lano kinetické</text><text x="294.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">FARMAKOKINETICKÉ interakce: vstřebávání (antacida a železo váží</text><text x="294.0" y="718.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">tetracykliny a chinolony), vazba na bílkoviny, metabolismus</text><text x="294.0" y="738.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(inhibice a indukce CYP450), vylučování (probenecid a penicilin).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="765.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="771.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Lano dynamické</text><text x="294.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">FARMAKODYNAMICKÉ interakce: synergie (alkohol + benzodiazepiny =</text><text x="294.0" y="791.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">útlum dechu; ACE inhibitor + kalium šetřící diuretikum =</text><text x="294.0" y="811.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">hyperkalemie) nebo antagonismus (β-blokátor ruší β2-agonistu u</text><text x="294.0" y="831.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">astmatu).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="859.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="865.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="864.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Grapefruit</text><text x="294.0" y="864.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Inhibuje střevní CYP3A4 → prudce zvýší hladinu statinů, blokátorů</text><text x="294.0" y="884.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">kalciových kanálů a imunosupresiv.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="911.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="917.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="916.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Třezalka</text><text x="294.0" y="916.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Silný induktor CYP3A4 a P-glykoproteinu → sníží účinnost</text><text x="294.0" y="937.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">kontracepce, warfarinu, cyklosporinu, digoxinu a antiretrovirotik.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="964.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="970.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="969.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Warfarin uprostřed</text><text x="294.0" y="969.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Nejrizikovější dvojice: warfarin s čímkoli · statin s makrolidem</text><text x="294.0" y="989.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nebo azolem · NSA s antikoagulanciem.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1017.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1023.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1022.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Prodloužené QT</text><text x="294.0" y="1022.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Látky prodlužující QT se sčítají: antiarytmika III. třídy,</text><text x="294.0" y="1042.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">makrolidy, chinolony, antipsychotika, ondansetron.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1069.7" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1075.7" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1074.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Nákupní taška</text><text x="294.0" y="1074.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Nejvíc interakcí nevzniká z léků na předpis, ale z toho, co pacient</text><text x="294.0" y="1095.0" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nehlásí: volně prodejná NSA, doplňky stravy a bylinky.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1121.4" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M35.3,1123.2 L865.2,1120.6 Q875.1,1121.5 872.4,1127.6 L874.6,1176.2 Q873.4,1185.5 864.7,1182.1 L31.1,1186.1 Q24.4,1188.3 26.6,1177.4 L25.8,1128.3 Q28.1,1120.2 30.3,1120.0" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M30.0,1119.7 L866.7,1118.9 Q873.1,1117.9 875.7,1129.4 L872.0,1180.7 Q872.3,1184.4 866.0,1186.4 L28.7,1188.0 Q29.2,1187.3 25.2,1175.7 L26.1,1127.2 Q24.4,1121.5 31.2,1121.9" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1142.4" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1142.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Serotonergní léčiva se také sčítají — SSRI, IMAO, tramadol, triptany a linezolid mohou spolu vyvolat</text><text x="66.0" y="1161.6" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">serotoninový syndrom.</text></svg>

fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ULTRARYCHLÝ metabolizátor odbourá léčivo dřív, než stačí zabrat —</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">LÉČBA SELHÁVÁ.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="823.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="829.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="828.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Obrácená cedule</text><text x="294.0" y="828.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">U PROLÉČIVA je to naopak: ultrarychlý CYP2D6 vytvoří z kodeinu</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nebezpečně mnoho morfinu, pomalý nemá z kodeinu ani z klopidogrelu</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">žádný účinek.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="896.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="902.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Laboratorní výsledek</text><text x="294.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Testuje se HLA-B*5701 před abakavirem, HLA-B*5801 před</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">allopurinolem, TPMT před azathioprinem a 6-merkaptopurinem, G6PD</text><text x="294.0" y="941.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">před primachinem a sulfonamidy.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="969.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="975.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Prasklý erytrocyt</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Deficit G6PD vede k HEMOLÝZE po primachinu, sulfonamidech a</text><text x="294.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nitrofurantoinu.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1021.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1027.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Horečka na sále</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">MALIGNÍ HYPERTERMIE po sukcinylcholinu a halogenovaných</text><text x="294.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">anesteticích — geneticky podmíněná idiosynkrazie; antidotem</text><text x="294.0" y="1067.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">dantrolen.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1094.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1100.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Izoniazid</text><text x="294.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">NAT2 určuje rychlost acetylace izoniazidu — pomalí acetylátoři mají</text><text x="294.0" y="1120.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">vyšší riziko neuropatie.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1146.5" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M29.7,1147.8 L865.4,1143.5 Q872.6,1148.7 871.4,1151.0 L873.9,1202.9 Q871.6,1212.5 867.5,1210.1 L34.7,1210.0 Q25.8,1213.2 27.2,1201.1 L28.0,1151.9 Q23.2,1143.0 31.6,1146.0" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M32.1,1143.1 L864.7,1148.3 Q870.9,1148.8 873.1,1148.6 L874.5,1201.9 Q875.6,1209.4 870.0,1207.8 L34.3,1208.6 Q28.6,1206.8 25.9,1206.7 L25.6,1154.2 Q28.4,1142.2 32.2,1146.2" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1167.5" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1167.5" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Farmakogenetika vysvětluje idiosynkrazii — neobvyklou reakci, která nesouvisí s dávkou ani s</text><text x="66.0" y="1186.7" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">mechanismem účinku a není imunitně podmíněná.</text></svg>

fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="723.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Pomalu klesající křivka</text><text x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">TOLERANCE — na týž účinek je třeba stále vyšší dávka; buduje se dny</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">až týdny. Farmakodynamická (down-regulace receptorů) nebo</text><text x="294.0" y="763.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">farmakokinetická (indukce vlastního metabolismu — barbituráty).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="790.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="796.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Vybitá baterie</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">TACHYFYLAXE — velmi rychlý pokles účinku při opakovaném podání v</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">krátkém sledu, typicky vyčerpáním zásoby mediátoru (efedrin).</text><text x="294.0" y="836.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ZVÝŠENÍ DÁVKY NEPOMŮŽE.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="863.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="869.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Náplast sundaná na noc</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Tachyfylaxe vzniká i u nitrátů — proto se ponechává noční interval</text><text x="294.0" y="889.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">bez nitrátu.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="916.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="922.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Bakterie za štítem</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">REZISTENCE je vlastnost MIKROORGANISMU, ne pacienta — primární</text><text x="294.0" y="941.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(přirozená) nebo získaná (mutace, přenos plazmidu). Nepleť si ji s</text><text x="294.0" y="962.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">tolerancí.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="989.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="995.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Zkřížené šipky</text><text x="294.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ZKŘÍŽENÁ TOLERANCE existuje mezi látkami se stejným mechanismem —</text><text x="294.0" y="1014.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">alkohol, benzodiazepiny a barbituráty; morfin a ostatní opioidy.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1042.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1048.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Spící pacient</text><text x="294.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">U opioidů roste tolerance rychle na euforii a útlum dechu.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1074.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1080.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Zácpa, která zůstává</text><text x="294.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Na zácpu a miózu po opioidech tolerance prakticky nevzniká — proto</text><text x="294.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">se laxativum podává po celou dobu léčby.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1126.1" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M35.1,1126.7 L864.7,1126.2 Q875.3,1124.1 875.0,1133.0 L874.1,1181.0 Q875.8,1191.0 867.0,1190.8 L31.4,1191.4 Q28.9,1190.5 26.8,1183.7 L28.9,1132.5 Q28.8,1122.2 30.5,1123.0" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M30.6,1127.2 L866.6,1127.6 Q872.6,1125.4 874.7,1128.1 L874.3,1183.1 Q875.8,1186.8 870.0,1191.1 L31.0,1191.9 Q26.6,1192.1 24.7,1184.8 L26.0,1133.0 Q25.9,1124.2 30.0,1127.5" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1147.1" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1147.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Tolerance na různé účinky téže látky se vyvíjí různě rychle — proto pacient s tolerancí na útlum</text><text x="66.0" y="1166.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">dechu může být stále ohrožen zácpou a miózou.</text></svg>

KASKÁDA: metoklopramid vyvolá parkinsonské projevy →</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nasadí se antiparkinsonikum. Blokátor kalciových kanálů vyvolá</text><text x="294.0" y="763.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">otoky → nasadí se diuretikum.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="790.9" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="796.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Zúžená ledvina</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Renální insuficience — snížit dávku léčiv vylučovaných ledvinami</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(aminoglykosidy, digoxin, metformin, lithium).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="843.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="849.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Slabé srdce</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Srdeční selhání snižuje průtok játry i ledvinami; u léčiv s vysokou</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">jaterní extrakcí hladina stoupá.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="896.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="902.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Pět krabiček</text><text x="294.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">POLYPRAGMAZIE — zpravidla 5 a více léčiv současně; roste riziko</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">interakcí, pádů, hospitalizací a klesá adherence.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="948.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="954.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Koště</text><text x="294.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">DEPRESKRIPCE — plánované vysazení léků, které už nepřinášejí</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">užitek; u polymorbidních stejně důležitá jako nasazení.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1001.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1007.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Astmatik s β-blokátorem</text><text x="294.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Kontraindikace bývají dané přidruženou chorobou: β-blokátor u</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">astmatu, NSA u renální insuficience a vředové choroby, metformin</text><text x="294.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">při riziku laktátové acidózy.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1074.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1080.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Otazník nad novým</text><text x="64.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">příznakem</text><text x="294.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Nový příznak je nežádoucí účinek léku, dokud se neprokáže opak —</text><text x="294.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">teprve pak se hledá nová diagnóza.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1126.1" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M33.6,1122.8 L869.1,1122.2 Q871.4,1127.2 872.0,1128.8 L871.7,1181.5 Q874.4,1187.2 864.7,1192.5 L34.4,1187.9 Q23.3,1191.1 23.6,1184.8 L22.9,1130.0 Q24.7,1126.2 32.0,1125.3" 866.4,1189.5 L29.8,1191.2 Q25.8,1188.5 26.7,1184.1 L22.6,1131.1 Q29.2,1124.5 30.0,1123.0" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1147.1" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1147.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Polypragmazie není jen počet léků, ale i jejich vzájemná zbytečnost — cílem je nejmenší počet léčiv,</text><text x="66.0" y="1166.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">který splní léčebný záměr.</text></svg>
