font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Šuplík A</text><text x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">TYP A (Augmented) — zesílený farmakologický účinek: závisí na</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">dávce, je předvídatelný a častý. Krvácení po warfarinu,</text><text x="294.0" y="763.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">hypoglykemie po inzulinu, bradykardie po β-blokátoru. Řeší se</text><text x="294.0" y="783.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">snížením dávky.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="811.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="817.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Šuplík B s kostkou</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">TYP B (Bizarre) — nesouvisí s dávkou ani s mechanismem, nedá se</text><text x="294.0" y="836.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">předvídat; vzácný, ale často závažný. Alergie, aplastická anemie po</text><text x="294.0" y="856.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">chloramfenikolu, maligní hypertermie, Stevensův–Johnsonův syndrom.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="884.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="890.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="889.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Hodiny</text><text x="294.0" y="889.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">TYP C — chronický, při dlouhém podávání (osteoporóza po</text><text x="294.0" y="909.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">kortikoidech).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="936.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="942.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="941.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Staré hodiny</text><text x="294.0" y="941.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">TYP D — pozdní: teratogenita, kancerogenita.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="969.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="975.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Bouchnuté dveře</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">TYP E — po náhlém vysazení: rebound hypertenze, adrenální</text><text x="294.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">insuficience, rebound nespavost.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1021.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1027.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Schránka hlášení</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Podezření se hlásí SÚKL; u nově registrovaných léčiv (černý</text><text x="294.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">trojúhelník) i podezření běžná — právě tak se odhalí to, co ve fázi</text><text x="294.0" y="1067.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">III nevyšlo najevo.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1094.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1100.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Zápis do dokumentace</text><text x="294.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Typ B znamená lék vysadit, už nikdy nepodat a poznamenat do</text><text x="294.0" y="1120.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">dokumentace.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1146.5" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M30.7,1143.4 L871.3,1144.5 Q872.0,1145.0 872.0,1153.7 L875.3,1205.0 Q874.6,1210.9 869.2,1207.5 L32.3,1209.1 Q28.7,1207.0 29.0,1204.4 L29.0,1153.5 Q27.4,1142.3 28.9,1147.9" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M31.6,1142.7 L865.5,1147.5 Q875.4,1143.7 873.8,1153.7 L874.5,1202.3 Q875.0,1211.7 867.3,1210.8 L28.7,1211.1 Q24.7,1212.9 25.3,1202.1 L24.6,1151.1 Q24.1,1148.5 31.6,1143.1" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1167.5" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1167.5" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Rozdělení A/B rozhoduje o postupu: typ A se řeší dávkou, typ B vysazením — proto je nutné je odlišit</text><text x="66.0" y="1186.7" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">dřív, než se sáhne po úpravě dávkování.</text></svg>

bronchospasmus, ANAFYLAXE</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">během minut.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="823.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="829.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="828.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Adrenalinové pero</text><text x="294.0" y="828.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">U anafylaxe je lékem první volby ADRENALIN INTRAMUSKULÁRNĚ do</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">stehna; antihistaminika a kortikosteroidy jsou jen doplňkové a</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">působí příliš pomalu.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="896.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="902.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Typ II a III</text><text x="294.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Typ II cytotoxický, typ III imunokomplexový (sérová nemoc).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="928.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="934.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="933.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Ekzém na ruce</text><text x="294.0" y="933.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Typ IV pozdní buněčný — kontaktní ekzém, makulopapulózní exantém za</text><text x="294.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">dny.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="981.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="987.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="986.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Idiosynkrazie</text><text x="294.0" y="986.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Geneticky podmíněná neobvyklá reakce BEZ účasti imunity — hemolýza</text><text x="294.0" y="1006.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">při deficitu G6PD, maligní hypertermie po sukcinylcholinu,</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">prodloužená apnoe při atypické pseudocholinesteráze.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1054.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1060.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1059.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Ampicilin u mononukleózy</text><text x="294.0" y="1059.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Exantém po ampicilinu u infekční mononukleózy NENÍ alergie — a</text><text x="294.0" y="1079.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nesmí se tak zapsat.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1105.8" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M29.6,1105.3 L866.7,1104.9 Q874.5,1106.9 872.0,1112.0 L875.4,1166.3 Q873.0,1171.9 867.0,1172.1 L35.0,1171.5 Q28.3,1172.2 23.5,1163.2 L29.2,1110.6 Q24.8,1103.7 34.7,1105.6" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1126.8" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1126.8" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Nauzea po antibiotiku není alergie, ale nesnášenlivost — přesto se často zapíše jako alergie a</text><text x="66.0" y="1146.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">pacient pak zbytečně přijde o celou lékovou skupinu.</text></svg>

Q876.0,159.9 874.4,168.4 L875.9,620.2 Q874.9,628.7 865.3,627.7 L34.0,630.8 Q27.2,626.8 27.1,618.8 L27.9,167.7 Q26.0,159.3 33.6,156.4" fill="none" stroke="#23303A" stroke-width="2.4" font-weight="700" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">CO JE VE SCÉNĚ</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="692.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="698.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Zlomená šroubovice</text><text x="294.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">MUTAGENITA — poškození genetické informace: genové mutace,</text><text x="294.0" y="718.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">chromozomové aberace, změny počtu chromozomů. V zárodečných buňkách</text><text x="294.0" y="738.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">se přenáší na potomstvo.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="765.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="771.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Nádor</text><text x="294.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">KARCINOGENEZE má fáze INICIACE (nevratné poškození DNA), PROMOCE</text><text x="294.0" y="791.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(podpora množení poškozeného klonu) a PROGRESE.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="818.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="824.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="823.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Dlouhé přesýpací hodiny</text><text x="294.0" y="823.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Latence je roky až desetiletí — proto se karcinogenita odhalí až po</text><text x="294.0" y="843.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">letech užívání a žádná registrační studie ji nezachytí.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="871.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="877.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="876.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Amesův test</text><text x="294.0" y="876.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Testuje se předklinicky: Amesův test (mutagenita na bakteriích),</text><text x="294.0" y="896.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">testy chromozomových aberací, dlouhodobé studie na hlodavcích.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="923.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="929.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="928.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Cytostatikum</text><text x="294.0" y="928.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Alkylační látky vyvolávají sekundární leukemie — lék na nádor</text><text x="294.0" y="949.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">zvyšuje riziko nádoru druhého.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="976.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="982.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="981.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Imunosuprese</text><text x="294.0" y="981.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Imunosupresiva zvyšují výskyt lymfomů a kožních nádorů.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1008.7" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1014.7" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1013.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Cigareta</text><text x="294.0" y="1013.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Genotoxické karcinogeny nemají bezpečnou dávku; negenotoxické</text><text x="294.0" y="1034.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">(hormonální, imunosupresivní) působí nepřímo — estrogeny bez</text><text x="294.0" y="1054.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">gestagenu a karcinom endometria.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1080.7" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M33.3,1078.6 L868.0,1077.2 Q874.9,1082.0 876.4,1088.6 L874.5,1137.9 Q876.0,1145.3 865.4,1143.1 L30.1,1144.9 Q25.8,1143.9 24.0,1139.5 L23.9,1088.4 Q29.3,1082.3 33.8,1077.9" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M33.2,1076.4 L867.8,1077.3 Q877.2,1079.3 876.6,1082.9 L875.0,1140.8 Q871.8,1141.2 866.1,1142.5 L35.2,1143.3 Q27.8,1142.3 26.0,1138.2 L28.3,1083.9 Q22.9,1082.3 35.0,1081.1" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1101.7" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1101.7" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">To, že lék léčí nádor a zároveň zvyšuje riziko jiného, není rozpor — je to poměr rizika a prospěchu,</text><text x="66.0" y="1121.0" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">který se váží u každého pacienta zvlášť.</text></svg>

y="723.9" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Vajíčko</text><text x="294.0" y="722.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">0.–2. TÝDEN — pravidlo „všechno, nebo nic“: buď zárodek zanikne,</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">nebo se poškození plně opraví.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="770.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="776.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Červené pole na kalendáři</text><text x="294.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">3.–8. TÝDEN — ORGANOGENEZE, období největšího rizika strukturních</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">vad.</text><circle cx="41.0" text-anchor="start">Isotretinoin a retinoidy, thalidomid, warfarin, valproát,</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">karbamazepin, fenytoin, metotrexát, mykofenolát, ACE inhibitory a</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">sartany, tetracykliny, živé vakcíny, inhibitory 5-α-reduktázy.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="896.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="902.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Rostoucí plod</text><text x="294.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Od 9. týdne vznikají FUNKČNÍ poruchy a poruchy růstu: ACE</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">inhibitory poškozují ledviny plodu, NSA předčasně uzavírají ductus</text><text x="294.0" y="941.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">arteriosus, tetracykliny barví zuby.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="969.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="975.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Kojení</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Do mléka přechází nejspíš látka lipofilní, málo vázaná na bílkoviny</text><text x="294.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">a zásaditá. Podává se ihned PO kojení. Kontraindikovány jsou</text><text x="294.0" y="1014.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">cytostatika, radiofarmaka, lithium, amiodaron.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1042.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1048.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Váhy rizika</text><text x="294.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Neléčená nemoc matky (epilepsie, astma, těžká deprese, infekce)</text><text x="294.0" y="1067.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">ohrožuje plod často víc než lék — neléčí se „nulovým rizikem“, ale</text><text x="294.0" y="1087.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">poměrem rizika a prospěchu.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1115.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1121.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1120.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Kyselina listová</text><text x="294.0" y="1120.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Podává se preventivně už před početím jako prevence rozštěpů</text><text x="294.0" y="1140.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">neurální trubice; u žen na antiepilepticích ve vyšší dávce.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1166.8" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M32.3,1169.1 L871.1,1169.0 Q870.7,1167.9 872.1,1169.6 L872.3,1225.6 Q871.6,1231.1 869.2,1233.0 L28.7,1229.6 Q26.5,1227.9 23.3,1223.0 L28.7,1172.4 Q24.9,1162.5 30.5,1167.2" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M30.4,1164.6 L866.1,1163.7 Q873.7,1164.2 870.9,1173.5 L873.5,1226.9 Q875.8,1232.2 865.9,1229.0 L31.2,1233.1 Q26.2,1232.4 28.0,1223.6 L29.3,1172.9 Q28.3,1164.0 34.8,1165.1" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1187.8" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1187.8" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Bezpečnost v graviditě se nedá odvodit z toho, že je lék volně prodejný — NSA jsou ve třetím</text><text x="66.0" y="1207.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">trimestru kontraindikované, přestože je má doma každý.</text></svg>

hmotnosti, u cytostatik na plochu</text><text x="294.0" y="743.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">povrchu těla — nikdy „zlomkem dávky dospělého“ od oka.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="770.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="776.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Kojenec</text><text x="294.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Novorozenec má nezralou glukuronidaci (gray baby syndrom po</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">chloramfenikolu), vyšší podíl tělesné vody, nižší vazbu na</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">bílkoviny a nezralou glomerulární filtraci.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="843.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="849.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Obarvený zub</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Tetracykliny do 8 let — zbarvení zubů a hypoplazie skloviny.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="875.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="881.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="880.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Poškozená chrupavka</text><text x="294.0" y="880.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Chinolony u dětí poškozují chrupavku.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="908.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="914.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="913.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Kodein</text><text x="294.0" y="913.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Kodein a tramadol — útlum dechu u ultrarychlých metabolizátorů</text><text x="294.0" y="933.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">CYP2D6; u dětí kontraindikované.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="960.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="966.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="965.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Reyeův syndrom</text><text x="294.0" y="965.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Kyselina acetylsalicylová u dítěte s horečnatým virovým onemocněním</text><text x="294.0" y="986.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">— Reyeův syndrom. Antipyretiky volby jsou paracetamol a ibuprofen.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1013.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1019.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1018.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Sirup a čípek</text><text x="294.0" y="1018.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Formy: perorální roztoky, sirupy, kapky, čípky; při dlouhodobé</text><text x="294.0" y="1038.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">léčbě pozor na obsah cukru a etanolu.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1065.1" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M29.4,1060.9 L866.6,1062.5 Q871.3,1063.6 872.1,1072.6 L875.8,1120.3 Q871.8,1126.0 866.9,1126.0 L33.4,1128.8 Q25.1,1128.6 28.8,1120.6 L28.0,1067.1 Q27.6,1063.5 34.2,1061.1" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M30.6,1061.4 L870.3,1060.9 Q873.2,1063.6 874.5,1066.8 L876.5,1122.8 Q875.4,1126.7 866.8,1128.3 L30.1,1131.0 Q25.1,1128.1 26.6,1119.6 L24.4,1070.9 Q23.2,1066.0 29.5,1063.3" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1086.1" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1086.1" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Řada léčiv nemá u dětí registraci a podává se off-label podle odborných doporučení — klinické studie</text><text x="66.0" y="1105.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">na dětech jsou vzácné.</text></svg>

cy="770.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="776.5" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Ledvina</text><text x="294.0" y="775.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Glomerulární filtrace klesá s věkem i při NORMÁLNÍM sérovém</text><text x="294.0" y="795.9" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">kreatininu, protože ubývá svalové hmoty — počítá se clearance, ne</text><text x="294.0" y="816.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">jen kreatinin.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="843.5" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Citlivý mozek</text><text x="294.0" y="848.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Vyšší citlivost na benzodiazepiny, opioidy, anticholinergika a</text><text x="294.0" y="868.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">antipsychotika — riziko pádů, zmatenosti a deliria.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="896.2" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="902.2" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Tuková zásoba</text><text x="294.0" y="901.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Ubývá svalu a přibývá tuku, takže lipofilní léčiva se hromadí a</text><text x="294.0" y="921.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">mají delší poločas.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="948.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="954.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Seznam Beers</text><text x="294.0" y="953.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Beersova kritéria a STOPP/START vyjmenovávají u seniorů nevhodná</text><text x="294.0" y="974.2" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">léčiva (dlouhodobě působící benzodiazepiny, anticholinergika, NSA)</text><text x="294.0" y="994.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">i ta, která chybějí.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1021.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1027.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Anticholinergní zátěž</text><text x="294.0" y="1026.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Sčítá se z mnoha léků najednou (antihistaminika I. generace,</text><text x="294.0" y="1047.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">tricyklika, spasmolytika, některá antipsychotika) — zmatenost,</text><text x="294.0" y="1067.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">retence moči, zácpa, pády.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1094.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1100.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Otazník</text><text x="294.0" y="1099.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Nový příznak u seniora je nežádoucí účinek léku, dokud se neprokáže</text><text x="294.0" y="1120.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">opak — jinak vzniká preskripční kaskáda.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1146.5" width="846.0" height="43.3" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M29.2,1142.9 L870.2,1147.0 Q873.0,1146.9 875.1,1153.5 L871.9,1183.5 Q874.5,1189.4 opacity="0.8"/><path d="M34.7,1147.6 L871.2,1143.8 Q875.8,1146.4 875.7,1151.5 L876.9,1186.7 Q876.0,1194.1 865.6,1188.3 L29.8,1192.8 Q25.3,1187.8 23.7,1184.4 L25.1,1151.6 Q27.7,1147.6 34.1,1143.1" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1167.5" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" často přinese větší užitek.</text></svg>

font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">1</text><text x="64.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Koncovka -mab</text><text x="294.0" y="697.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">MONOKLONÁLNÍ PROTILÁTKA. Předposlední slabika říká původ: -xi-</text><text x="294.0" y="718.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">chimérická (infliximab), -zu- humanizovaná (trastuzumab), -u- plně</text><text x="294.0" y="738.5" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">lidská (adalimumab).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="765.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="771.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">2</text><text x="64.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Koncovka -cept</text><text x="294.0" y="770.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">FÚZNÍ BÍLKOVINA, solubilní receptor, který na sebe naváže cytokin:</text><text x="294.0" y="791.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">etanercept váže TNF-α, dále abatacept, aflibercept.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="818.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="824.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">3</text><text x="64.0" y="823.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Koncovka -nib</text><text x="294.0" y="823.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">MALÁ MOLEKULA, inhibitor kinázy — na rozdíl od protilátek se podává</text><text x="294.0" y="843.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">PERORÁLNĚ (imatinib, tofacitinib, ibrutinib).</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="871.1" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="877.1" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">4</text><text x="64.0" y="876.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Injekce, ne tableta</text><text x="294.0" y="876.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Protilátky jsou bílkoviny — v trávicím traktu by se strávily, proto</text><text x="294.0" y="896.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">se podávají parenterálně a vyžadují chladový řetězec.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="923.8" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#28556E" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="929.8" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="middle">5</text><text x="64.0" y="928.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#28556E" text-anchor="start">Podobná, ne stejná kopie</text><text x="294.0" y="928.8" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">BIOSIMILAR — u bílkoviny nelze vyrobit přesnou kopii jako u</text><text x="294.0" y="949.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">generika; dokládá se srovnatelná kvalita, účinnost a bezpečnost, ne</text><text x="294.0" y="969.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">pouhá bioekvivalence.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="996.7" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1002.7" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">6</text><text x="64.0" y="1001.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Latentní tuberkulóza</text><text x="294.0" y="1001.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Před anti-TNF-α je nutné vyloučit latentní tuberkulózu a hepatitidu</text><text x="294.0" y="1022.1" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">B — hrozí reaktivace.</text><circle cx="41.0" cy="1049.4" r="15" fill="#FFFDF7" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="41.0" y="1055.4" font-size="17" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">7</text><text x="64.0" y="1054.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="700" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">Protilátky proti léku</text><text x="294.0" y="1054.4" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">Imunogenicita — tvorba neutralizujících protilátek proti léku,</text><text x="294.0" y="1074.7" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">které ho časem přestanou nechat účinkovat. Dále infuzní a</text><text x="294.0" y="1095.0" font-size="15.4" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">hypersenzitivní reakce.</text><rect x="27.0" y="1121.4" width="846.0" height="62.5" rx="10" fill="#F7CFC4" opacity="0.42"/><path d="M32.1,1120.0 L871.0,1121.8 Q874.4,1119.9 872.2,1125.5 L872.7,1181.7 Q877.2,1187.3 869.1,1182.2 L33.9,1182.1 Q23.3,1186.4 28.6,1175.6 L27.5,1124.2 Q27.7,1117.6 30.0,1117.9" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><path d="M30.1,1117.6 L865.9,1117.9 Q875.5,1117.0 874.8,1123.6 L874.1,1176.6 Q875.6,1184.6 869.9,1183.9 L30.8,1184.4 Q25.3,1182.6 27.2,1178.7 L24.3,1124.3 Q22.7,1118.3 35.3,1120.2" fill="none" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="2.0" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"/><text x="40.0" y="1142.4" font-size="15" font-weight="400" fill="#23303A" text-anchor="start">⚠️</text><text x="66.0" y="1142.4" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">Inhibitory kontrolních bodů (nivolumab, pembrolizumab) působí opačně než imunosupresiva — uvolňují</text><text x="66.0" y="1161.6" font-size="14.6" font-weight="400" fill="#5E1F14" text-anchor="start">brzdu imunity, a proto jejich nežádoucí účinky vypadají jako autoimunitní choroby.</text></svg>
